OrderGrad optimizes finite-sample L-statistics, i.e., weighted averages of sorted rewards or costs, recovering objectives such as VaR, CVaR, trimmed means, medians, and top-m/best-of-K criteria by changing only the rank weights. For any fixed sample size and rank-weight vector, OrderGrad provides an unbiased gradient estimator for the corresponding order-statistic objective. The method is implemented as a simple reward transformation that can then be used in an otherwise standard policy-gradient or reparameterized update. We study the resulting estimator's variance behavior and evaluate it on tasks where mean optimization is mismatched to the deployment objective, including LLM math post-training and other tasks. OrderGrad provides a unified, plug-and-play route to risk-averse, robust, and exploratory learning.
  Code: https://github.com/paavo5/ordergrad

</details>


### [186] [A Sliced-Wasserstein Framework on Correlation Matrices for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06104)
*Chen Hu,Rui Wang,Jiale Zhou,Jingjun Yi,Shaocheng Jin,Yidong Song,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于拉回欧几里得度量切片 Wasserstein (PEMSW) 的通用框架，并在全秩相关矩阵流形上实例化了两种相关性切片 Wasserstein (CorSW) 差异（基于 Off-Log Metric 和 Log-Scaled Metric），进而构建了用于 EEG 解码的领域泛化框架。在三个 EEG 数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分布偏移下具有更好的泛化能力，且训练开销低、推理零成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 解码中常用的协方差描述子对通道缩放敏感，而全秩相关矩阵作为尺度不变替代方案具有优势。现有研究缺乏在相关矩阵流形上定义合适度量的通用框架，且 EEG 解码面临跨域分布偏移的挑战。

Method: 1) 提出 PEMSW 通用框架，在具有拉回欧几里得度量的流形上定义切片 Wasserstein 差异；2) 在该框架下基于 OLM 和 LSM 两种相关几何，实例化 CorSW 差异；3) 基于 CorSW 构建 EEG 解码的领域泛化框架。

Result: 在三个 EEG 数据集上，该方法在分布偏移场景下实现了更好的泛化性能，且训练开销低，不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 所提的 CorSW 差异及相应的领域泛化框架能有效提升 EEG 解码在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，是一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) offers noninvasive, millisecond resolution recordings of neuronal activity and is widely used in neuroscience and healthcare. Many EEG decoding pipelines rely on covariance descriptors for their robustness to noise, but such representations are sensitive to channel-wise scaling. Recent studies have therefore advocated full-rank correlation matrices as a scale-invariant alternative for EEG decoding. In this paper, we propose a general framework for Sliced Wasserstein (SW) discrepancies on manifolds endowed with Pullback Euclidean Metrics (PEMs), termed Pullback Euclidean Metric Sliced Wasserstein (PEMSW). Within this framework, we instantiate two Correlation Sliced-Wasserstein (CorSW) discrepancies on the manifold of full-rank correlation matrices under two recently introduced correlation geometries, \textit{i.e.}, the Off-Log Metric (OLM) and Log-Scaled Metric (LSM). Building on CorSW, we further develop a domain generalization (DG) framework for EEG decoding. Experiments on three EEG datasets demonstrate improved generalization under distribution shifts, with low training overhead and no additional inference cost. The source code is available at https://github.com/ChenHu-ML/CorSW.

</details>


### [187] [Adaptive state-action abstractions via rate-distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06123)
*Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态调整强化学习抽象粒度的原则：当学习误差与抽象误差相当时，细化抽象。通过值误差分解（Bellman残差+双模拟度量）实现切换策略，并基于率失真原理构建可连续调整分辨率的软状态-动作抽象。在表格环境中验证了高压缩比下仍能保持近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽有多种构建简化任务的技术，但缺乏动态调整抽象粒度的通用原则。婴儿学步时先解决粗粒度问题（保持直立、够到照顾者），只在粗粒度学习收益递减时才细化问题，受此启发提出自动化原则。

Method: 1) 提出性能证书，将值误差分解为学习误差界（Bellman残差）和抽象误差界（双模拟度量）；2) 当学习误差与抽象误差可比时触发细化；3) 基于率失真原理构建软状态-动作抽象，沿状态和动作轴连续调整分辨率。

Result: 在多种表格设置中验证，表明即使对状态和动作信息进行大幅有损压缩，仍能实现接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于误差分解动态调整抽象粒度的原则，通过率失真软抽象实现，在大幅压缩下保持近最优性能，为强化学习中的自动抽象粒度调整提供了理论基础。

Abstract: When learning to walk, infants seem to address a coarse version of the problem first - stay upright, reach the caregiver - and refine it only when further practice at that resolution stops paying off. Reinforcement learning offers multiple techniques for building simple versions of complex tasks, but lacks general principles for how to dynamically adjust the granularity of these abstractions during learning. This paper proposes one such principle: refine the abstraction as soon as the learning error within it becomes comparable to the error induced by the abstraction itself. Here, we investigate one way of formalising this principle via a performance certificate that decomposes value error into two terms: a learning error bound captured by a Bellman residual, and an abstraction error bound given by a bisimulation metric. The resulting switching strategy is implemented by soft state-action abstractions built from rate-distortion principles, whose resolution along state and action axes can be continuously adjusted. We validate this construction in a range of tabular settings, showing that near-optimal performance can be achieved under substantial lossy compression of state and action information.

</details>


### [188] [Tight list replicability bounds via a novel sphere covering theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06148)
*Ari Blondal,Hamed Hatami,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过证明一个新的拓扑球面覆盖定理（源于Borsuk-Ulam定理），研究了列表可复现性框架中列表大小与精度参数及假设类复杂度度量之间的sharp界，得到了VC类的精确界以及大间隔半空间的最优列表大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表可复现性是形式化学习理论中可复现性的重要框架，核心问题在于理解列表大小如何依赖于精度参数和假设类的自然复杂度度量，但此前缺乏sharp的界。

Method: 证明了一个新的拓扑球面覆盖定理：若d-球面被开集覆盖且每个开集位于一个开半球内，则至少有d+1个开集有公共交集。基于该定理推导VC类和大间隔半空间的列表可复现性界。

Result: 得到了VC类上列表大小与精度的sharp界；证明大间隔半空间在间隔不太大时最优列表大小等于环境维度；当间隔非常大时，设计了可复现算法实现最小列表大小⌈d/2⌉+1。

Conclusion: 拓扑球面覆盖定理为列表可复现性提供了理论工具，在VC类和大间隔半空间上取得了精确的列表大小界，揭示了列表大小与几何/拓扑结构之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In recent years, list replicability has emerged as a framework for formalizing reproducibility in learning theory. A central question is how the required list size relates to the accuracy parameter and natural complexity measures of the hypothesis class.
  To achieve sharp bounds on list replicability, we prove a novel topological sphere covering theorem, derived from the Borsuk-Ulam theorem. Specifically, if the $d$-sphere is covered by open sets, each of which lies in an open hemisphere, then $d+1$ of these sets must have a common intersection. Using this result, we obtain a sharp bound on the relationship between list size and accuracy for VC classes. We also show that for large-margin half-spaces, provided the margin is not too large, the optimal list size equals the ambient dimension. However, when the margin is taken to be very large, we devise a replicable algorithm achieving the minimal list size of $\lceil d/2 \rceil + 1$.

</details>


### [189] [Trust-Aware Predictive Emissions Monitoring for Gas Turbine Fleets with Limited Labelled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06156)
*Rebecca Potts,Aiden Durrant,Rick Hackney,Georgios Leontidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向燃气轮机机队的信任感知概率框架，在仅有少量标注数据的情况下实现NOx排放预测，通过多模块协同生成可解释的信任分数，有效识别不可靠预测，高置信度子集MAE从0.202降至0.070。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的预测排放监测系统在燃气轮机机队部署时面临标签稀疏问题——仅有少量资产有排放标注，难以保证未标注设备上预测的可靠性，亟需一种能评估预测可信度的方法。

Method: 构建了一个多模块信任感知框架：(1)多头递归预测模型，(2)学习置信度估计，(3)集成不确定性量化，(4)辅助特征预测，(5)特征空间距离分析，(6)运行范围诊断。在标注数据上校准各信号，生成每个样本的可解释信任分数。

Result: 基于置信度的过滤将MAE从全覆盖的0.202降至最高置信度10%预测的0.070；未标注和分布外样本表现出更高的不确定性和更低的置信度，表明框架能有效响应分布偏移。

Conclusion: 该信任框架为未标注涡轮机上的排放预测提供了可操作的可靠性信息，支持工业机队中PEMS更透明、可信的部署。

Abstract: Machine learning-based predictive emissions monitoring systems offer a practical alternative to direct emissions measurement, but their deployment across gas turbine fleets is challenging when emissions labels are available for only a small subset of assets. In this work, a trust-aware probabilistic framework is proposed for fleet-level gas turbine NOx prediction under limited labelled supervision. The framework combines a multi-head recurrent prediction model with learned confidence estimation, ensemble-based uncertainty quantification, auxiliary feature prediction, feature-space distance analysis, and operating-range diagnostics. These signals are calibrated on labelled data to produce interpretable per-sample trust scores, providing indicators of prediction reliability on unlabelled turbines, supporting the identification of predictions that should be treated with greater caution during fleet-level deployment. Confidence-based filtering reduces MAE from 0.202 at full coverage to 0.070 for the highest-confidence 10\% of predictions, demonstrating that confidence estimates are meaningfully related to prediction error. Unlabelled and out-of-distribution samples exhibit increased uncertainty and reduced confidence, indicating that the framework responds appropriately to distributional shift. The results show that the proposed trust framework provides actionable reliability information for emissions prediction on unlabelled turbines, supporting more transparent and trustworthy deployment of PEMS across industrial fleets.

</details>


### [190] [On the training of physics-informed neural operators for solving parametric partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06164)
*Nanxi Chen,Chuanjie Cui,Airong Chen,Sifan Wang,Rujin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了物理信息神经算子（PINO）训练中的关键组件（架构设计、优化器选择、损失平衡、配点采样策略），实验表明CViT架构表现最优，并发现PINO训练中存在与PINN类似的梯度冲突和因果违反等优化问题，且PINN的缓解算法仍有效。精心设计的PINO训练管道可匹配甚至超越纯数据驱动神经算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINO的训练效率和鲁棒性尚未被充分理解，存在研究空白。本文旨在系统性地分析PINO训练流程中的关键组件，以弥补这一认知差距。

Method: 选取三种代表性算子骨干（DeepONet、FNO、CViT），在五个不同的参数化PDE系统上进行实验，系统评估架构设计、优化器选择、损失平衡和配点采样策略对PINO训练的影响。

Result: CViT在所有基准测试中表现稳定且优异；PINO训练中存在与PINN类似的梯度冲突和因果违反等优化病理现象；PINN的缓解算法在PINO中仍然有效；精心设计的物理信息训练管道在某些情况下可匹敌甚至超越纯数据驱动方法。

Conclusion: 提供了PINO训练优化挑战的系统性实证理解，并为高效鲁棒的物理信息算子学习提出了实用的训练管道建议。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural operators (PINOs) aim to learn solution operators for partial differential equations by using the governing physics as supervision, rather than relying solely on paired input-output simulation data. By incorporating physical constraints into the training objective, PINOs combine the cross-instance generalization of neural operators with the data efficiency of physics-informed learning. Despite this promise, how to train PINOs efficiently and robustly remains less well-understood than the training of either data-driven neural operators or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). To bridge this gap, we examine key components of the PINO training pipeline, including architecture design, optimizer choice, loss balancing, and collocation-point sampling strategy. We study three representative operator backbones, Deep Operator Network (DeepONet), Fourier Neural Operator (FNO), and Continuous Vision Transformer (CViT), across five diverse parametric PDE systems. Our results show that CViT provides consistently strong and stable performance across the considered benchmarks. Beyond architecture, we find that several optimization pathologies previously identified in PINN training naturally arise in PINOs, including gradient conflicts and causal violation. We also find that mitigation algorithms developed for PINNs remain effective in the PINO setting. We further compare physics-informed and data-driven training under different data regimes, revealing that a carefully designed physics-informed training pipeline can match, and in some cases, outperform purely data-driven neural operators. Taken together, these findings provide a systematic empirical understanding of the optimization challenges in PINO training and inform a practical pipeline for efficient and robust physics-informed operator learning. Code and data are available at https://github.com/NanxiiChen/PI-CViT.

</details>


### [191] [Learning to model pediatric asthma exacerbation from multiple risk factors: a case study in coastal Virginia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06174)
*Jonathan Colen,Eric Werner,Maryam Golbazi,Heather Richter,Diana McSpadden,Amy Quinn,Jocel Santos,Mary Jane Darling,Mary Margaret Gleason*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了广义线性模型、神经网络和稀疏字典学习三种方法，用于预测弗吉尼亚州汉普顿地区儿童哮喘急性发作的风险，并识别空气污染、气象和社区社会经济因素之间的交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童哮喘是一种常见疾病，受到空气污染、气象条件和社区社会经济因素的共同影响，在大规模时空数据中建模哮喘急性发作需要厘清多个贡献因素之间的复杂关系。

Method: 收集2018-2023年弗吉尼亚州汉普顿地区7个城市超过150万人口的空气污染测量、天气数据和社区机会指标，在邮编代码层面建模儿童哮喘急性发作就诊数据。比较了广义线性模型（GLM）、神经网络（NN）和基于稀疏字典学习的框架三种方法。

Result: 比较了各模型的预测性能，估计了输入暴露变量对哮喘急性发作的相对风险，发现不同框架之间结果具有一致性。

Conclusion: 本研究连接了统计模型和可解释机器学习模型，揭示了可能影响哮喘急性发作的协同交互作用，为未来指导弗吉尼亚沿海地区公共卫生干预措施提供了基础。

Abstract: Childhood asthma is a common illness exacerbated by air pollution as well as meteorological and neighborhood-level socioeconomic factors. Modeling asthma exacerbation (AE) in large spatiotemporal datasets requires disentangling impacts from multiple contributors. In this case study, we compared three techniques that balance predictive power with interpretability to predict AE in Hampton Roads, a coastal Virginia region comprising 7 cities and over 1.5 million people. After collating ambient air pollution measurements, weather data, and measures of neighborhood opportunity, we modeled zip code-level acute AE visits to a regional children's hospital and affiliated providers from 2018-2023. Generalized linear models (GLM) provided a baseline while neural networks (NN) served as a maximally predictive target. To bridge between statistical models and deep learning, we developed a framework based on sparse dictionary learning to identify and interpret parsimonious nonlinear interacting equations. After comparing each model's predictive performance, we estimated relative risks for AE due to input exposure variables and found consensus across frameworks. Our work links statistical and interpretable machine learning models to highlight possible synergistic interactions influencing AE, and may enable future studies to guide public health interventions in coastal Virginia.

</details>


### [192] [Learning to Route LLMs from Implicit Cost-Performance Preferences via Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06178)
*Jiahao Zeng,Ming Tang,Ningning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaRouter框架，通过元学习实现个性化LLM路由，在少量交互中学习用户隐式偏好，以在性能和成本之间取得最优平衡，实验表明其在分布内外任务上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由方法无法适应不同用户对成本和性能的个性化偏好，缺乏用户中心的优化范式。

Method: 将用户偏好建模为上下文赌博机中的不同任务，提出MetaRouter元学习框架，通过少量交互高效学习用户隐式偏好，实现偏好感知的LLM路由。

Result: MetaRouter在分布内和分布外任务上均优于强基线，且在学习效率、对路由LLM变化的鲁棒性以及多模型路由可扩展性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 基于元学习的个性化路由框架能有效解决异构用户需求下的成本-性能优化问题，具有良好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) present a trade-off between performance and cost, where more powerful models incur greater expense. LLM routing aims to mitigate expenses while maintaining performance by sending queries to the most suitable model. However, existing methods cannot perform well for different user cost-performance preferences. To address this gap, we introduce a novel perceptive LLM routing paradigm for personalized and user-centric cost-performance optimization, which efficiently learns users' implicit preferences through little interaction. To handle the challenge of heterogeneous user needs, we formulate preference profiles as a set of distinct tasks in contextual bandit and propose MetaRouter, a meta-learning framework designed for preference-aware LLM routing. Experimental results show that MetaRouter outperforms strong baselines on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks. Furthermore, it exhibits high efficiency in learning user preferences, robustness to changes in the routable LLMs, and scalability to multi-model routing.

</details>


### [193] [A Machine Learning-Based Framework for Discovering Huntington's Disease Stages: Integrating Graph Representation Learning and clustering to Uncover Progression Dynamics in Longitudinal Enroll-HD Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06196)
*Lubna M. Abu Zohair,Marta Vallejo,MD Azher Uddin,John R. Woodward,Hind Zantout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动态图表示学习的无监督机器学习框架，通过在低维潜在空间中对纵向临床测量数据进行聚类，识别出亨廷顿病（HD）的四种有意义的疾病阶段，这些阶段具有明确的临床测量边界且重叠最小，优于传统的临床分期方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的亨廷顿病临床分期框架依赖预定义的临床测量阈值和专家决策，这些离散截断值可能掩盖有意义的阶段内变异，且易受评估者间差异影响，特别是在运动和功能评估方面。

Method: 基于动态图表示学习从纵向临床测量中捕获患者内部和患者间的时间关系，利用学习到的表示进行K-means++聚类，通过稳定性分析迭代增加聚类数以发现有意义的阶段。

Result: 在302名受试者（1,477次访视）的Enroll-HD队列中，该框架使用四维潜在空间实现了稳健的聚类性能，通过聚类稳定性分析识别出四个有统计学意义的疾病阶段，每个阶段对应明确的临床测量边界，与既有方法相比重叠最小。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动的方法能够更精确地捕捉亨廷顿病的自然临床进展，为疾病分期提供了更客观、一致的框架，有助于理解病程、患者分组、个性化护理和治疗发现。

Abstract: Huntington's disease (HD) is a progressive brain disorder that gradually affects movement, cognitive function, and behavior. Identifying the stage of the disease accurately and consistently is important for understanding its course, grouping patients, personalized care, and discovering treatment. Existing clinical staging frameworks rely primarily on predefined clinical measurement thresholds and clinical expert decisions, yet these discrete cut-offs may obscure meaningful intra-stage variability and remain vulnerable to inter-rater differences, especially in motor and functional assessments. To address these limitations, we developed an unsupervised machine learning framework based on dynamic graph representation learning to capture temporal relationships within and across patients from longitudinal clinical measurements. Using the learned representations, we applied K-means++ clustering to identify well-separated groups. We then iteratively increased the number of clusters (k), using stability analysis to assess robustness and reveal additional meaningful clusters beyond the initial optimal solution. We applied the framework to 302 individuals from the Enroll-HD cohort (1,477 visits, 44 clinical variables per visit; 80% manifest participants), enabling data-driven discovery of HD stages reflecting natural clinical progression. Despite the limited cohort size, the proposed framework achieved robust clustering performance using a four-dimensional latent space, identifying four meaningful and statistically distinct disease stages through clustering stability analysis. Each stage corresponded to well-defined clinical measurement boundaries, with minimal overlap compared to previously established clinical staging methods.

</details>


### [194] [Non-Negative Matrix Factorization for Event Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06205)
*Raphaël Romero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventNMF，一种直接作用于连续时间事件数据的非负矩阵分解模型，无需预先分箱或平滑处理，通过泊松过程与非负B样条基分解事件强度，能恢复可解释的时间模板，在理论和实验上均优于传统分箱方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMF在分析连续时间事件数据时需要先进行分箱或平滑预处理，这可能导致实体级异质性和细粒度时间特征丢失。作者希望设计一种直接对原始事件时间建模的NMF方法，避免信息损失，同时保持计算高效和解释性。

Method: EventNMF将每个实体的事件建模为泊松过程，其强度函数通过非负B样条基与实体-因子权重矩阵的乘积进行因子分解。模型使用简单的估计过程（如乘法更新）恢复共享的潜在时间模板和实体特定的激活权重，直接在事件时间上优化似然函数，无需分箱预处理。

Result: 理论分析表明标准分箱计数方法是EventNMF在零阶样条下的特例；通过偏差-方差权衡分析揭示了方法特性；在合成潜变量模型上的实验验证了有效性；在多个真实世界应用（如神经科学、地震学、社交网络）中展示了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: EventNMF是一种理论上严谨、实现简单、计算高效的连续时间NMF方法，通过直接在事件时间上建模避免了预处理阶段的信息损失，可广泛应用于各类连续时间事件数据中的可解释结构发现。

Abstract: Continuous-time event data, in which entities emit instantaneous events over time, arises naturally across many domains such as neuroscience, seismology, and social networks. Non-negative matrix factorization (NMF) is a natural tool to uncover interpretable structure in such data, but it has so far only been applied after binning or smoothing the entity-level counting measures. This preprocessing step comes with the risk of erasing entity-level heterogeneities and fine-grained temporal features. In this paper, we introduce EventNMF, a continuous-time non-negative factorization model that operates directly on event times: each entity's events are modeled as a Poisson process whose intensity factorizes through a non-negative B-spline basis, and a simple estimation procedure recovers interpretable temporal templates shared across entities. The resulting method is mathematically principled, easy to implement, and computationally efficient. We further show that standard binned-count approaches arise as the special case of degree-zero splines, explore bias-variance tradeoffs and compare against existing methods on a synthetic latent factor model, and demonstrate the effectiveness of EventNMF on several real-world applications.

</details>


### [195] [Design a Reliable LLM-Integrated Interface for Mortality Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06235)
*Thi Kim Ngan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成大语言模型（LLM）的死亡率预测接口，通过自然语言输入驱动确定性预测管道，在保持统计效力和可重复性的同时，大幅提升了非专家用户的可访问性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 死亡率预测在精算与政策决策中至关重要，但现有实现技术复杂，非专业用户难以使用。本工作旨在通过LLM降低使用门槛，同时不牺牲模型的统计严谨性和可重复性。

Method: 采用三阶段方法：1）基于CoMoMo包构建基线预测管道，复现已有死亡率预测结果；2）扩展管道以支持滚动原点评估和多步预测，使用均方误差（MSE）进行评估；3）设计原型接口，利用本地LLM作为受约束的编排层，将自然语言输入转换为结构化配置，驱动确定性预测流程。

Result: 系统证明LLM可以在不损害可重复性、透明性或精算有效性的前提下，增强高风险分析工作流程的可访问性。

Conclusion: LLM作为约束编排层可以有效桥接自然语言交互与确定性预测管道，为非专家用户提供易于使用的死亡率预测工具，同时保持精算领域的专业标准。

Abstract: Mortality forecasting plays an important role in actuarial and policy decision-making, but its implementation remains technically complex and inaccessible to non-expert users. This project proposes a reliable large language model (LLM)-integrated interface that improves usability while maintaining statistical power. The LLM is designed as a constrained orchestration layer that translates natural-language inputs into structured configurations for a deterministic forecasting pipeline. A three-phase methodology is employed to ensure accuracy, usability, and transparency. First, a baseline pipeline is implemented using the CoMoMo package, reproducing established mortality forecasting results. Second, the pipeline is extended to generate multi-step forecasts using rolling-origin evaluation and mean squared error (MSE). Third, a prototype interface uses a local LLM to handle users' forecasting requests in plain language. The system demonstrates that LLMs can enhance accessibility without compromising reproducibility, transparency, or actuarial validity in high-stakes analytical workflows.

</details>


### [196] [Tracing the Oracle: Improving Diffusion Timestep Scheduling for 3D CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06236)
*Yujia Wu,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TrO框架，通过动态规划优化扩散模型逆问题求解中的时间步调度，在少量采样步数下显著提升3D CT重建的保真度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在3D CT逆问题推理中计算开销大，且均匀时间步调度无法捕捉反向条件扩散SDE的非均匀演化，导致较大截断误差。

Method: TrO框架：在少量样本上密集采样数值积分轨迹作为参考oracle，利用动态规划全局最小化少步近似与oracle之间的累积误差，从而优化时间步调度，将有限采样步精确分配给易受截断误差影响的关键演化阶段。

Result: 在AAPM数据集多个3D CT重建任务中，结合DDS方法，优化后的时间步在不超过10步的严格预算下，显著提升重建保真度和计算效率，优于现有启发式调度。

Conclusion: TrO是一种即插即用的时间步调度优化框架，能有效解决扩散模型在3D CT逆问题中截断误差大和计算效率低的问题。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models demonstrate impressive potential in solving highly ill-posed 3D computed tomography (CT) inverse problems, while the inference process suffers from significant computational overhead. Furthermore, existing uniform timestep schedules fail to capture the non-uniform evolution of the reverse conditional diffusion stochastic differential equation, thereby introducing substantial truncation errors. To overcome this limitation, we propose Tracing the Oracle (TrO), a plug-and-play framework for improved timestep scheduling. Specifically, we treat densely sampled numerical integration trajectories on a few samples as the reference oracle. The optimized schedule is extracted by leveraging dynamic programming to globally minimize the cumulative error between the few-step approximation and the oracle. This mechanism precisely allocates the limited sampling steps to critical evolution stages that are highly susceptible to truncation errors. Our extensive experiments on the AAPM dataset across multiple 3D CT reconstruction tasks demonstrate that, when combined with the state-of-the-art 3D CT reconstruction method DDS, our optimized timesteps significantly improve reconstruction fidelity and computational efficiency compared to existing heuristic schedules, especially under a strict budget of no more than 10 sampling steps.

</details>


### [197] [Generative Criticality in Large Language Model Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06238)
*Huajian Ruan,Jinyang Li,Xingyu Guo,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统计场框架，将LLM的token嵌入视为一维链上的连续自旋变量，通过定义磁化率和序参量，发现softmax温度T变化时在特征T_c附近出现尖锐的磁化率峰值、序参量突变、语义方向坍缩，以及内在维度在T_c处达到极小值，现象类似连续相变但生成过程非平衡，提供了量化分析LLM集体统计结构的新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏从统计力学视角系统理解LLM文本生成的集体统计结构，特别是温度参数如何影响输出文本的统计特性尚未被深入探索。

Method: 将token嵌入视为连续自旋变量，定义基于连通两点关联函数的磁化率和基于系综平均嵌入场的序参量，通过变化softmax温度T观察上述量的行为，并用TwoNN方法估计内在维度进行独立验证。

Result: 在特征温度T_c附近观察到：(1) 尖锐的磁化率峰值且呈幂律标度行为；(2) 序参量发生快速变化；(3) T_c以下坍缩到单一语义方向；(4) 内在维度在T_c附近达到最小值。这些结果在Qwen3模型（0.6B-32B）和多种提示类别上均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该统计场框架为探测LLM输出的集体统计结构提供了定量工具，表明解码策略与临界现象之间存在潜在联系，但自回归生成的非平衡特性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We propose a statistical-field framework for text generated by large language models (LLMs), treating token embeddings as continuous spin variables on a one-dimensional chain. Defining a susceptibility from the connected two-point correlator and an order parameter from the ensemble-averaged embedding field, we vary the \texttt{softmax} temperature $T$ and observe a sharp susceptibility peak near a characteristic $T_c$ with power-law-like scaling, a concurrent rapid change in the order parameter, and a collapse onto a single semantic direction below $T_c$. The intrinsic dimension estimated by the two nearest neighbor (TwoNN) method independently corroborates these findings, reaching a minimum near $T_c$. Results are robust across model scales (Qwen3: 0.6B--32B) and prompt categories. While the phenomenology closely resembles a continuous phase transition, the non-equilibrium nature of autoregressive generation warrants further investigation. Our framework provides quantitative tools for probing the collective statistical structure of LLM outputs and suggests connections between decoding strategies and critical phenomena.

</details>


### [198] [Your GFlowNet Secretly Learns an Optimal Transport Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06272)
*Ian Maksimov,Nikita Morozov,Denis Belomestny,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了非无环GFlowNet与最优传输（OT）之间的理论联系，证明最小流GFlowNet在固定初始流分布后，其目标函数可简化为具有图诱导最短路径代价的Kantorovich OT问题。最优解下，学习的GFlowNet策略编码了从源分布到目标分布的最优传输计划，且采样轨迹可恢复最优耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立GFlowNet与最优传输之间的理论联系，将GFlowNet学习框架扩展到大规模图上的OT问题。

Method: 证明最小流GFlowNet目标函数在固定初始流分布后等价于Kantorovich OT问题，通过边流和神经参数化实现大规模图上的OT求解。

Result: 与精确OT求解器结果一致，GFlowNet能够学习高质量的传输计划。

Conclusion: GFlowNet框架可有效应用于大规模图上的最优传输问题，采样的轨迹能够恢复最优耦合。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) are a framework for sampling structured objects via stochastic trajectories in a directed graph. In this work, we establish a theoretical connection between non-acyclic GFlowNets and optimal transport (OT). We show that fixing the initial flow distribution in a minimum-flow GFlowNet reduces its objective to a Kantorovich OT problem with graph-induced shortest path costs. At the optimum, the learned GFlowNet policy therefore encodes an optimal transport plan from the source distribution to the target distribution: we show that sampling trajectories from the minimum-flow GFlowNet recovers the corresponding optimal coupling. Our formulation enables applying the GFlowNet learning framework to OT problems on large graphs via edge flows and neural parameterization. Experiments confirm agreement with exact OT solvers and demonstrate that GFlowNets can learn high-quality transport plans.

</details>


### [199] [PAC-Bayesian Adversarially Robust Generalization for Message Passing Graph Neural Networks: A Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06293)
*Ziling Liang,Xinping Yi,Qingsong Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了敏感性感知的PAC-Bayesian框架至消息传递图神经网络，通过Jacobian对齐的敏感度矩阵和各向异性高斯后验，导出了更紧的对抗鲁棒泛化界，将主导维度因子从隐藏宽度依赖项降低为类别数K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络对抗鲁棒泛化分析多依赖各向同性高斯后验且在完整参数空间控制权重扰动，无法捕捉异构参数敏感性且受隐藏宽度依赖复杂度项制约，导致泛化界不够紧。

Method: 1) 推导不同参数块对网络输出的Jacobian矩阵，量化参数敏感性；2) 利用K类图分类中Jacobian矩阵秩最多为K的性质，构建Jacobian对齐的敏感度矩阵；3) 使用优化协方差矩阵的各向异性高斯后验，紧致上界KL散度；4) 将谱范数依赖从隐藏宽度项缩减至类别数K。

Result: 得到了更紧的MPGNN对抗鲁棒泛化保证，主导维度因子从隐藏宽度依赖项降低为类别数K，显著提升了泛化界的紧致性。

Conclusion: 该方法为图神经网络对抗鲁棒性设计提供了理论指导，验证了敏感性感知框架在MPGNN中的有效性。

Abstract: Whilst the vulnerability of graph neural networks (GNNs) to adversarial attacks poses a critical threat to graph representation learning, the understanding of the robust generalization behavior remains a fundamental challenge in the adversarial setting. Recently, PAC-Bayesian margin-based generalization analysis substantially advances this line of research by providing a flexible and data-dependent analytical framework. However, existing robust analyses often rely on isotropic Gaussian posteriors and control weight perturbations in the full parameter space, which limits the ability to capture heterogeneous parameter sensitivity yet hinges on hidden-width-dependent complexity terms, resulting in not-tight-enough generalization bounds. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed sensitivity-aware PAC-Bayesian framework from deep neural networks to message passing GNNs (MPGNNs) and derive a tighter robust generalization bound in the adversarial setting. Specifically, we first quantify how sensitive the perturbations across different parameter blocks are to the network outputs by deriving the output Jacobians with respect to the weight parameters. Exploiting the fact that these Jacobian matrices have rank at most $K$ in $K$-class graph classification, we then construct Jacobian-aligned sensitivity matrices and use anisotropic Gaussian posteriors with optimized covariances to upper bound the KL divergence in a tight way. Notably, by refining the spectral-norm dependence on the learned weights and reducing the leading dimension factor from hidden-width-dependent terms to the number of classes $K$, our analysis yields much tighter robust generalization guarantees for MPGNNs, thereby guiding their designs to enhance adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [200] [Reactive Flux Matching: Mechanism Discovery and Adaptive Sampling of Rare Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06295)
*Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes,Nicholas M. Boffi,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flux Matching框架，从反应轨迹数据中学习电流速度u(z)和标量势h(z)，分别用于识别主反应路径和作为数据驱动反应坐标，无需动力学或稳态分布先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径采样方法能生成连接亚稳态的反应轨迹集合，但从这些数据中提取机制性见解仍然困难，现有方法（如基于committor的方法）在非马尔可夫集体变量投影下表现不佳。

Method: 通过加权亥姆霍兹-霍奇分解反应电流，学习电流速度u(z)（流线追踪主反应路径）和标量势h(z)（作为反应坐标）；两者最小化反应路径系综上的二次泛函，类似于生成建模中的flow matching损失。

Result: Flux Matching在分子系统中通过生成电流速度轨迹和速率常数计算得到验证。

Conclusion: Flux Matching提供了一种不依赖committor、对非马尔可夫投影鲁棒的反应路径分析方法，可自适应生成改进采样的界面。

Abstract: Path sampling methods generate ensembles of reactive trajectories connecting metastable states, but extracting mechanistic insight from these data remains nontrivial. We introduce Flux Matching, a framework that learns two complementary objects directly from reactive trajectory data: a current velocity $u(z)$, whose streamlines trace the dominant reaction pathways, and a scalar potential $h(z)$, obtained from a weighted Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition of the reactive current, that serves as a data-driven reaction coordinate. Both minimize quadratic functionals over the reactive path ensemble, analogous to the flow matching loss in generative modeling, and require no knowledge of the underlying dynamics or stationary distribution. Unlike committor-based methods, $u$ and $h$ remain well-defined under projection onto non-Markovian collective variables, and their level sets in turn provide adaptive interfaces for improved sampling with enhanced sampling methods. Flux Matching is validated through the generation of current velocity trajectories and rate constant calculations on molecular systems.

</details>


### [201] [Tangram: Unlocking Non-Uniform KV Cache for Efficient Multi-turn LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06302)
*Hyungmin Kim,Minsoo Kim,Hongseok Kim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tangram是一个面向多轮LLM服务中非均匀KV缓存压缩的系统，通过确定性预算分配、头组分页和预加载均衡三项技术，在完全保持模型精度的前提下，实现最高2.6倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM服务中KV缓存线性增长对GPU内存和带宽造成巨大压力，非均匀压缩虽能保留更多信息，但引入内存碎片化、调度复杂性和内核利用率降低等系统性问题，导致现有服务系统效率显著下降。

Method: (1) 确定性预算分配：基于每个注意力头的固有模式分配静态内存占用，消除动态调度开销和预填充阻塞；(2) 头组分页：将具有相似保留需求的注意力头聚类，使用独立的向量化页表管理，最大化物理内存回收；(3) 预加载负载均衡：利用静态预算配置文件确保GPU均匀利用，无运行时开销。

Result: 与现有基线相比，吞吐量提升最高达2.6倍，且完全保持模型精度。

Conclusion: Tangram通过系统层面的创新设计，有效解决了非均匀KV缓存压缩带来的系统性挑战，使得非均匀KV缓存方案在实际部署中变得实用可行。

Abstract: Multi-turn Large Language Model (LLM) serving is critical for consistent user experiences, yet the linear growth of the Key-Value (KV) cache imposes significant pressure on GPU memory and bandwidth. Non-uniform KV compression effectively preserves more information by considering the individual importance of each KV cache. However, such KV cache heterogeneity introduces various systemic challenges - including memory fragmentation, scheduling complexities, and diminished kernel utilization - which collectively lead to significant inefficiencies in existing LLM serving systems.
  To overcome these challenges, we present Tangram, a novel serving system designed to make Non-uniform KV caches practical. Tangram addresses systemic inefficiencies through three core techniques: (1) Deterministic Budget Allocation assigns a static memory footprint to each head based on its intrinsic pattern, entirely eliminating dynamic scheduling overhead and prefill stalls; (2) Head Group Page clusters attention heads with similar retention demands and manages them with independent, vectorized page tables, thereby maximizing physical memory reclamation; and (3) Ahead-of-Time (AOT) Load Balancing leverages static budget profiles to ensure uniform GPU utilization without runtime overhead. Experimental results show that Tangram improves throughput by up to 2.6x compared to existing baselines, while fully preserving model accuracy. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/aiha-lab/TANGRAM.

</details>


### [202] [Plug-and-Play Guidance for Discrete Diffusion Models via Gradient-Informed Logit Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06303)
*Hongkun Dou,Zike Chen,Fengji Li,Hongjue Li,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GILC框架，通过重用预训练去噪网络作为变分代理，以即插即用方式实现离散扩散模型的可控生成，无需重新训练，在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的可控生成面临计算开销大或需要重新训练的问题，且高维离散空间中的梯度不稳定导致现有方法效果受限。

Method: 提出Gradient-Informed Logit Correction (GILC)框架，通过将预训练去噪网络重用作变分代理来估计引导信号；引入无雅可比机制直接校正干净预测logits，避免高维离散空间中的梯度不稳定问题；支持可微和不可微奖励函数。

Result: 在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上，GILC达到SOTA性能，且无需额外训练，经常优于微调方法。

Conclusion: GILC是一种高效、即插即用的离散扩散模型可控生成框架，通过梯度信息引导logits校正，有效解决了离散空间中的引导信号估计问题。

Abstract: Controllable generation with discrete diffusion models is often hindered by high computational overhead or the need for retraining. In this paper, we present \underline{\textbf{G}}radient-\underline{\textbf{I}}nformed \underline{\textbf{L}}ogit \underline{\textbf{C}}orrection (\textbf{GILC}), a plug-and-play framework that efficiently estimates guidance signals by repurposing the pretrained denoising network as a variational proxy. To circumvent the gradient instability inherent in high-dimensional discrete spaces, we introduce a Jacobian-free mechanism that directly corrects the clean prediction logits, facilitating stable and effective guidance. Our method accommodates both differentiable and non-differentiable reward functions. Extensive experiments across DNA, protein sequence, and molecular generation tasks demonstrate that GILC achieves state-of-the-art performance without additional training, frequently outperforming fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [203] [Learning What to Forget: Improving LLM Unlearning via Learned Token-Level Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06320)
*Gizem Yüce,Giorgos Nikolaou,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ATWU框架，通过保留冲突准则联合学习令牌级遗忘权重和模型参数，在无需外部监督的情况下实现自回归语言模型的高效机器遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法忽略或依赖外部信息来估计遗忘样本中各令牌的相关性差异，缺乏统一的、不依赖外部监督的令牌级遗忘重要性度量准则。

Method: 将令牌权重和模型参数联合优化形式化，基于保留目标冲突度定义令牌的遗忘特异性；提出ATWU框架，利用隐藏状态上的线性评分器联合学习令牌权重和模型参数。

Result: 在TOFU和RWKU基准上达到最优的遗忘-保留权衡，超越样本级方法、概率加权启发式和辅助模型方法；学习的遗忘分数与真实遗忘跨度对齐更好。

Conclusion: 保留冲突是识别语言模型应遗忘内容的有效准则，支持从模型表示中无监督学习令牌级遗忘特异性，且计算开销极小。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted knowledge from a trained model while preserving its general capabilities. For autoregressive language models, not all tokens in a forget sample are equally relevant to forgetting. Existing approaches either ignore this heterogeneity or rely on auxiliary models, heuristics, or external annotations to estimate each token's relevance for forgetting. We instead characterize it through the interaction with the retain objective: a token is forget-specific to the extent that minimizing the forget loss on that token does not conflict with retain optimality. We formalize this perspective as a joint optimization problem over the model parameters and the token weights and show that, under a natural separation condition, the resulting objective recovers the oracle forget-specific token support. Motivated by this formulation, we introduce Alternating Token-Weighted Unlearning (ATWU), a lightweight framework that jointly learns token forget-specificity and model parameters during unlearning using a simple linear scorer over the hidden states, without external token level supervision. Across TOFU and RWKU, ATWU achieves state of the art forget-retain trade-offs, outperforming sample-level methods, probability-based token weighting heuristics, and auxiliary-model-based approaches. Moreover, the learned scores align substantially better with ground truth forget-specific spans, indicating that ATWU identifies semantically meaningful token level forgetting signals. Overall, our results suggest that retain conflict provides an effective criterion for identifying what language models should forget, enabling unsupervised learning of token level forget-specificity directly from model representations with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [204] [PAMF: Prior-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Incomplete Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06328)
*Ziwen Kan,Wugeng Zheng,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAMF框架，通过先验感知流匹配和权重共享，显式处理多模态时间序列中两种缺失模式（模态内缺失和模态级缺失），将插补与下游预测耦合，在多个医疗基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过掩码或缺失嵌入隐式表示缺失数据，缺乏实例级缺失信息学习，且大多仅针对单一缺失模式设计；插补方法对不同缺失模式统一处理，且插补与下游任务隔离，无法引导插补生成更具信息性的表示。

Method: 提出PAMF框架：(1) 使用类型特定的先验初始化流匹配源状态，区分两种缺失类型；(2) 通过架构匹配的编码器和权重共享连接插补与分类，将任务相关表示传递给插补过程。

Result: 在多个多模态医疗时间序列基准上，PAMF在多种数据集和缺失设置下取得了最优的整体下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 显式处理结构不同的缺失模式并将插补与下游预测耦合能有效提升多模态时间序列任务性能。

Abstract: In healthcare, multimodal time series tasks often operate on incomplete observations in practice, for example when ECG segments are lost because electrodes detach or an entire respiratory channel is unavailable during overnight monitoring. Such missingness typically appears in two structurally distinct patterns: within-modality missing, where values are absent within an otherwise observed modality, and modality-level missing, where an entire modality is unavailable. Existing methods typically represent unobserved data implicitly through masks or missing embeddings, without learning instance-specific missing information, and most are designed for only one missingness pattern. A natural approach is to explicitly estimate the missing data; however, existing imputation methods treat missingness uniformly despite their different structural priors, and the imputation process is often isolated from downstream tasks, preventing downstream tasks from guiding imputation toward more informative representations. To address these limitations, we present PAMF, a multimodal time-series framework that explicitly handles different missingness patterns while coupling imputation with downstream prediction through prior-aware flow matching and weight sharing. Specifically, the method initializes the flow-matching source state with type-specific priors to distinguish two missing types. It further connects imputation and classification through architecturally matched encoders with weight sharing, transferring task-relevant representations into the imputation process. Experiments on multiple multimodal healthcare time-series benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves the strongest overall downstream performance across diverse datasets and missing settings compared with existing baselines.

</details>


### [205] [Efficient Mean Curvature Computation on High-Dimensional Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06329)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补方法将高维数据局部平均曲率计算的复杂度从O(m^4)降至O(k²m + kmp²)，实现了50-300倍加速，且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据集的局部平均曲率估计是几何感知机器学习算法（如MCBP）的关键，但原始实现需要构造形状算子矩阵H，其迹形式的计算复杂度为O(m^4)，当特征维度m较大时难以处理。

Method: 1) 利用协方差矩阵特征向量的正交性和迹算子的循环性推导出精确代数恒等式，消除矩阵H，将每点计算成本降至O(m^2)；2) 用k×m中心化数据矩阵的截断SVD替代满秩特征分解（复杂度O(k²m)），并对零空间特征向量贡献基于Haar测度期望进行解析近似，最终总成本为O(k²m + kmp²)。

Result: 在真实数据集上，相比原始实现实现了50-300倍的加速，且使用快速估计器替代原始版本时精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提方法将曲率建立为一种可扩展的数据驱动几何特征，可广泛应用于从经典到现代深度学习流水线的各类机器学习任务。

Abstract: Estimating local mean curvature at each point of a high-dimensional dataset is a key ingredient of geometry-aware machine learning algorithms, such as the Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP) method. The naive implementation of this computation, based on a local shape operator approximated from k-nearest neighbor patches, involves an explicit construction of a matrix $H$ whose trace form yields an $O(m^4)$ cost per point, rendering the approach intractable for datasets with more than a few dozen features. This paper introduces two complementary contributions that together reduce this cost by several orders of magnitude. The first contribution is an exact algebraic identity. This identity, derived from the orthogonality of the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix and the cyclicity of the trace operator, eliminates $H$ entirely and reduces the per-point cost to $O(m^2)$ after the eigendecomposition. The second contribution addresses the remaining $O(m^3)$ bottleneck of the full eigendecomposition. Since the local covariance matrix has rank at most $k-1 \ll m$, we replace it with a truncated SVD of the $k \times m$ centered data matrix, an $O(k^2 m)$ operation, and derive an analytical approximation for the contribution of the null-space eigenvectors based on the expected value of their outer product under the Haar measure. The resulting estimator has total cost $O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$, where $p = k-1$. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm speedups of 50 to 300 times relative to the original implementation, with negligible loss when the fast estimator is used to replace the original version. By providing a scalable and data-driven estimate of local curvature, the proposed method establishes curvature as a practical geometric feature for a broad range of machine learning tasks, from classical to modern deep learning pipelines.

</details>


### [206] [Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders for Effective Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06333)
*Seyed Arshan Dalili,Mehrdad Mahdavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出稀疏自编码器（SAE）将潜在特征假设为一维结构是错误的，这会导致特征分裂；作者提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器（SASA），使用学习到的解码器子空间替代单向量解码器，通过Top-s分组门控和核范数正则化实现块稀疏，从而将特征整合到单个组中，显著降低样本复杂度，实验表明SASA在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上减少了特征分裂与吸收，提升了单义性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE的每个潜在特征只分配一个解码器方向，隐含地假设特征是一维的。然而大语言模型中的特征往往具有多维结构，这种假设不匹配会导致特征分裂（feature splitting），即一个连贯特征被碎片化到多个近共线的潜在变量中，产生虚假的多重性并模糊内在几何结构。

Method: 1. 理论分析：从几何角度证明，用单方向解码器重构内在维度d_i≥2的特征到误差ε所需原子数随d_i指数增长；从优化角度证明，存在从真实d_i维基到更低风险的连续路径，梯度下降会驱动字典进入指数分裂区域。2. 提出SASA：用学习到的解码器子空间替代单向量解码器；采用Top-s分组门控实现块稀疏；引入核范数正则化自适应调整每个组的有效秩。3. 理论证明当块大小r≥d_i时，单个组能表示完整特征切片且是全局最优解，样本复杂度从指数级降至多项式级。

Result: 在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上的实验表明，SASA减少了特征分裂和吸收，提高了单义性和可解释性，并且在训练预算约为标准SAE一半的token数下，性能匹配或超越标准SAE。

Conclusion: SAE的一维假设与LLM特征的多维结构不匹配，是特征分裂的根本原因。SASA通过引入子空间解码器、块稀疏和核范数正则化，从理论和实践上解决了这一问题，为机械可解释性提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used for mechanistic interpretability in large language models, yet their formulation assigns each latent feature a single decoder direction, implicitly assuming features to be one-dimensional. We show that this assumption mismatches with the multi-dimensional structure of model features, provably inducing feature splitting through two distinct mechanisms. Geometrically, reconstructing a feature of intrinsic dimension $d_i \ge 2$ to error $\varepsilon$ with single-direction decoders forces a number of atoms that is exponential in $d_i$. From an end-to-end optimization perspective, this splitting is not merely possible but actively preferred. We prove that there exists a continuous path from the true $d_i$-dimensional basis to a strictly lower risk of the $\ell_1$-regularized SAE objective, whose descent directions drive any trained dictionary into that exponential regime. A single coherent feature is therefore fragmented across many near-collinear latents, producing spurious multiplicity and obscuring the intrinsic geometry. Motivated by this, we introduce Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders (SASA), which replace single-vector decoders with learned decoder subspaces, enforce block sparsity via Top-$s$ group gating, and adapt each group's effective rank with a nuclear-norm regularizer. We then show that once the block size satisfies $r \ge d_i$, a single group not only can represent the entire feature slice but is the global minimizer of the SASA objective. This consolidation yields a sample complexity polynomial in $d_i$ rather than exponential -- a decisive advantage given that every training activation costs an LLM forward pass. Empirically, on GPT-2 and Mistral-7B, SASA reduces feature splitting and absorption, improves monosemanticity and interpretability, and matches or exceeds standard SAEs while training on roughly half the token budget.

</details>


### [207] [Quantifying the Privacy of Counterfactuals by Leveraging Membership Inference Attacks Against Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06334)
*Maryam Babaei,Yingke Wang,Hadrien Lautraite,Heber H. Arcolezi,Ulrich Aivodji,Sebastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现反事实解释（counterfactuals）可被用于隐私攻击，借鉴合成数据上的成员推理攻击方法，在无需访问原始模型的情况下，仅凭反事实样本即可成功实施成员推理攻击，揭示发布反事实解释存在隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释常用于高风险决策领域解释模型，但其可能被恶意利用进行隐私攻击。现有针对反事实的成员推理攻击通常需要能够查询模型，本文旨在探索更现实、更危险的场景——仅凭反事实样本本身能否成功实施隐私攻击。

Method: 借鉴合成数据上的成员推理攻击方法，将其应用于各类反事实解释样本。实验设置包括两种场景：1）有模型访问权限的传统攻击；2）仅使用反事实样本、无模型访问权限的攻击。

Result: 成功证明了仅凭反事实样本（无需访问原始模型）即可实施有效的成员推理攻击，且多种类型的反事实解释均存在此隐私泄露风险。

Conclusion: 模型开发者在发布反事实解释时应更加谨慎，因为这可能导致训练数据的隐私泄露。

Abstract: Counterfactuals are typically used in high-stakes decision areas to explain a machine learning model by showing how changes to the user profiles result in the desired outcome. However, explaining the model's decisions through counterfactuals can also be exploited by an adversary to conduct privacy attacks against the model or its training data. Drawing on the analogy that counterfactuals provide realistic substitutes for real training data, similar to synthetic data, we demonstrate in this paper how it is possible to successfully perform privacy attacks on counterfactuals by drawing on the attacks developed against synthetic data. More precisely, we investigate the effectiveness of the membership inference attacks designed for synthetic data on various types of counterfactuals. Additionally, while existing membership inference attacks against counterfactuals usually require to be able to query the model, we show how it is possible to perform successful membership inference attacks using only a set of counterfactuals, with no access to the model from which they are generated. Our results demonstrate that model developers should be more cautious when releasing counterfactuals to various users, as it can lead to a privacy breach.

</details>


### [208] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果定位方法在蒸馏LLM（Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507）中识别出负责时间偏好（近期收益与长期后果权衡）的底层子图，发现该偏好主要位于模型的中上层节点，且LLM对人类未来折扣更缓、偏好不稳定，最后展示了通过转向向量可调控时间偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被部署于需要权衡短期收益与长期后果的决策场景，然而其内部如何表征和解决这种权衡尚不明确，因此需要对其时间偏好的内在机制进行因果解释。

Method: 使用梯度归因和激活补丁的汇聚证据，对蒸馏LLM进行因果定位，识别出负责时间偏好的底层子图（中上层节点）；通过残差流分析时间维度的几何编码；进行行为分析比较LLM与人类的折扣率差异及跨语境稳定性；最后通过转向向量进行时间偏好调控实验。

Result: LLM对未来折扣比人类更缓（即更倾向于长期收益），但这种偏好跨语境不稳定；在预期定位层中，残差流编码了时间维度的几何结构；转向向量可以改变时间偏好。

Conclusion: 可解释性机制分析有助于实现对LLM规划和推理中时间偏好的可靠控制，相比隐式依赖训练，显式控制更为必要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [209] [Bridging Domain Expertise and Generalization for Performance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06335)
*Shuxuan Li,Zhilin Zhao,Quyu Kong,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FRAP方法，通过融合基础模型与外部基础模型的预测分布，在分布偏移下更可靠地估计模型性能，无需真实标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅依赖单一模型的输出，分布偏移会放大模型偏差，削弱其与真实性能的相关性，需要更可靠的替代指标。

Method: 引入外部基础模型，通过温度缩放校准使其预测分布与基础模型对齐，再基于置信度加权融合两个模型的预测，构建更稳健的参考分布，以基础模型与该参考分布的一致性作为性能估计。

Result: 在多种数据集和架构上的实验表明，FRAP相比于现有代表性方法在分布偏移下取得了一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 融合基础模型的鲁棒性与基础模型的领域专长能有效提升分布偏移下的性能估计可靠性。

Abstract: Performance estimation under distribution shift aims to predict how a model behaves on an unlabeled test set whose distribution differs from the training data, a scenario that requires reliable indicators that can faithfully reflect model behavior without ground-truth labels. Existing approaches rely solely on the outputs of the given model whose biases are amplified once the distribution shifts, weakening the correlation with the true performance. Motivated by this limitation, we propose Fused Reference Alignment Prediction (FRAP), which leverages the complementary strengths of an external foundation model and the base model to construct a more reliable surrogate of the ground-truth labels. FRAP aligns the prediction distribution of the foundation model with that of the base model by applying temperature-scaled calibration that minimizes their divergence. The aligned predictions are fused through confidence-based weighting into a refined reference distribution that integrates robustness from the foundation model and domain-specific expertise from the base model, and performance estimation is obtained by measuring how closely the base model predictions agree with this reference. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and architectures show that FRAP provides consistent and substantial improvements over representative performance-estimation methods under distribution shift.

</details>


### [210] [Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06344)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等变神经信念传播（ENBP），一种因子图框架，其消息为等变高斯混合模型，能够合成秩-2精度矩阵，在GEOM-QM9/ Drugs上实现98.9%构象覆盖率、0.090Å误差和亚秒级延迟（比扩散基线快100倍以上），在多体机器人推理中在15+智能体场景下保持收敛，精度误差~10⁻⁷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变网络只能产生标量和向量，无法生成各向异性不确定性所需的秩-2精度张量；单分量消息会将多模态能量景观坍塌为物理无意义的平均值，无法处理空间嵌入变量的概率推理。

Method: 提出ENBP框架，消息为等变高斯混合模型，其充分统计量在SE(3)下精确变换；通过等变外积合成秩-2精度矩阵，经可微谱分解处理，并通过基于KL散度的贪心混合约简（可证明与SE(3)交换）保持可计算性。

Result: 在GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs上，ENBP达到98.9%构象覆盖率、0.090Å误差、亚秒级延迟，比扩散基线快100倍以上且精度更高；在多体机器人推理中，普通loopy BP在15+智能体时发散，ENBP收敛且碰撞率接近零，等变误差~10⁻⁷（增强基线为10⁻¹）。

Conclusion: ENBP通过等变高斯混合消息和秩-2精度矩阵合成，实现了对空间嵌入变量的高效概率推理，在分子构象生成和多体机器人推理任务上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic inference over spatially embedded variables requires beliefs that respect $SE(3)$ symmetry, yet existing equivariant networks produce only scalars and vectors -- not the rank-2 precision tensors needed for anisotropic uncertainty, and single-component messages collapse multi-modal energy landscapes to physically meaningless averages. We introduce Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework whose messages are equivariant Gaussian mixture models with sufficient statistics that transform exactly under $SE(3)$. Rank-2 precision matrices are synthesised via equivariant outer products, ingested through differentiable spectral decomposition, and kept tractable by a greedy KL-based mixture reduction that provably commutes with $SE(3)$. On GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs, ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage at 0.090 $\mathring{A}$ error with sub-second latency -- over $100\times$ faster than diffusion baselines at higher accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, vanilla loopy BP diverges at 15+ agents while ENBP converges with near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (${\sim}10^{-7}$ vs.\ $10^{-1}$ for augmented baselines).

</details>


### [211] [Performance Evaluation of GraphCast for Medium-Range Weather Forecasting over Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06348)
*Wolfgang R. Rowell,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GraphCast与ECMWF IFS HRES在巴西四个气候子区域的预报表现，发现GraphCast在冬季中程预报中对快速传播的斜压系统表现不佳，但在延伸期因平滑小尺度变率而具有优势；在夏季湿季则能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，同时抑制高频对流变率。这为巴西建立了MLWP模型评估基线，并指导未来模型的"热带化"优化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLWP模型在全球尺度表现优异，但在全球南方（如巴西）复杂高对流环境中的区域验证仍然稀缺，缺乏系统性评估基准。

Method: 采用可扩展的云原生流水线和WeatherBench-X框架，以IFS分析为基准真值，评估GraphCast与IFS HRES在巴西四个气候子区域中$T_{850}$、$Q_{850}$、$Z_{500}$等对流层变量在四个季节窗口的表现。

Result: GraphCast呈现依赖天气态度的技能分布：南半球冬季中程（2-7天）对$Z_{500}$的快速斜压系统预报欠佳，但延伸期因确定性指标下对混沌小尺度变率的固有平滑而获益；南半球夏季湿季则能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，同时抑制高频对流变率以改善确定性NWP温度预报。

Conclusion: 该研究为巴西建立了MLWP模型评估基线，明确了模型在复杂对流环境中的物理边界，为未来针对区域韧性的"热带化"优化提供了指导方向。

Abstract: The paradigm of global weather forecasting is rapidly shifting with the emergence of Machine Learning Weather Prediction models (MLWP). While these data-driven architectures demonstrate remarkable global skill, regional benchmarks in the Global South remain scarce, leaving their efficacy in complex, highly convective environments largely unverified. This study evaluates the performance of GraphCast operational against the deterministic ECMWF IFS HRES as baseline across four distinct Brazilian climatic sub-regions. Utilizing a scalable, cloud-native pipeline and the WeatherBench-X framework for benchmarking weather models, we assess selected tropospheric variables ($T_{850}$, $Q_{850}$, $Z_{500}$) over four selected seasonal windows, employing the operational IFS analysis as the ground truth to calculate the statistical metrics for both models. Results reveal a regime-dependent skill profile. During the austral winter, GraphCast underperforms in the medium range (lead days 2-7) for $Z_{500}$ when resolving fast-propagating baroclinic systems over southern Brazil, but regains an advantage in the extended range, where its inherent smoothing of chaotic small-scale variability becomes beneficial under deterministic skill metrics. Conversely, during the austral summer wet season, GraphCast accurately captures large-scale moisture transport while intrinsically dampening the high-frequency convective variability that degrades deterministic NWP temperature forecasts. These findings establish a baseline for Brazil and define the specific physical boundaries that will guide future ``tropicalization'' efforts, aiming to optimize these foundational AI models for regional resilience.

</details>


### [212] [Maximising the Set-Piece Return: Optimising Football Corner Tactics with Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06353)
*Sean Groom,Michael Groom,Francisco Belo,Axl Rice,Liam Anderson,Victor-Alexandru Darvariu,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图强化学习的角球战术优化方法，通过调整进攻方球员位置和速度最大化首次触球射门概率，在3000+英超角球数据上显著优于传统基线方法，实现了从历史模仿向奖励驱动的战术发现转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球战术评估方法局限于刻画历史动作或分析师指定的反事实场景，无法超越历史模式去发现新的、可泛化的球员配置和策略。

Method: 将角球战术优化建模为决策问题，采用图结构数据的强化学习架构，训练一个通用策略来调整任意初始球员位置和速度，以最大化首次触球射门概率。

Result: 在3000+英超角球数据上，该方法在匹配推理预算下显著优于基线优化技术。

Conclusion: 图强化学习能够将定位球分析从历史评估和模仿转向奖励驱动的战术发现。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly employed for the evaluation of football tactics. However, existing approaches focus on characterising historical actions or analyst-specified counterfactual scenarios. In this work, we seek to go beyond the imitation of historically observed patterns towards discovering new generalisable player configurations and strategies. To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.

</details>


### [213] [End-to-End Subgraph Detection with GraphDETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06364)
*Dexiong Chen,Till Hendrik Schulz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDETR是一个将子图检测建模为集合预测问题的深度学习框架，采用图神经网络编码目标图，并通过Transformer解码器与可学习查询向量，端到端地一次性预测所有模式实例。该方法不仅能处理精确结构匹配，还支持近似匹配，在最多1000节点的目标图中可检测最多50节点的模式，在ChEMBL分子官能团检测任务上达到AP₁₀₀=91.2的强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子图检测在科学领域至关重要，但其与NP完全的子图同构问题密切相关，传统组合方法仅适用于小规模模式或中等规模图，亟需高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 将子图检测转化为集合预测问题，使用GNN编码目标图，采用一组固定的可学习查询向量，通过Transformer解码器联合预测所有模式出现位置，并利用二分图匹配实现端到端训练。

Result: 可检测分子结构、环、团及模糊模式等多样化模式（最多50节点），目标图规模可达1000节点；在ChEMBL分子官能团检测任务上AP₁₀₀=91.2。

Conclusion: GraphDETR有效克服了传统组合方法的局限性，同时支持精确与近似匹配，在子图检测任务上展现出强大的可扩展性和性能。

Abstract: Subgraph detection seeks to identify whether and where instances of query patterns occur within a larger graph. This problem is fundamental across scientific domains and is closely related to subgraph isomorphism, which is NP-complete, limiting combinatorial approaches to small patterns or moderately sized graphs. We introduce GraphDETR, a deep learning framework that formulates subgraph detection as a set prediction problem, analogous to DETR in object detection. GraphDETR encodes the target graph with a graph neural network, and employs a fixed set of learnable query vectors, decoded via a transformer decoder, to predict all pattern occurrences jointly in a single forward pass. This is enabled by training the model end-to-end with bipartite matching. Unlike traditional combinatorial methods that only solve exact structural matching, GraphDETR naturally extends to approximate matching, enabling detection beyond exact pattern correspondence. Empirically, we show that GraphDETR can detect diverse patterns, such as molecular structures, cycles, cliques, and fuzzy patterns of up to 50 nodes, in target graphs with up to 1000 nodes. We further evaluate on molecular functional group detection over the ChEMBL dataset, where GraphDETR predicts the complete set of functional groups per molecule, achieving a strong performance of $\text{AP}_{100} = 91.2$.

</details>


### [214] [Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06385)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Mahdi Abdelguerfi,Julian Simeonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨数据集评估方法，用于HEC-RAS 2D中基于学习原生细胞替代模型的求解器一致水面高程预测。引入LRFIO（学习响应场惯性算子），一种无强制、基于增量的学习替代模型，通过封闭形式原生细胞展开实现高效预测，在四个基准测试中取得最大验证遗憾4.30%，并实现最高2.75×10^4倍于HEC-RAS的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有替代模型在评估时常受限于栅格重映射误差和信息访问混杂问题，未能直接在原始非均匀计算单元上进行求解器一致的评估，且缺乏对模型复杂度增加是否合理的严格验证。

Method: 提出LRFIO，一种无强制、基于增量的学习替代模型，从已求解的HEC-RAS轨迹中校准惯性响应算子，并通过封闭形式原生细胞展开部署。评估采用基线优先响应层次（持久性、全局标定惯性、分段响应场惯性），将分段、残差修正和神经化惯性作为可学习建模选择，仅当验证证据支持时才保留额外复杂度。

Result: 跨越四个不同HEC-RAS 2D基准测试，LRFIO对不同领域保留不同响应结构，展现自适应学习复杂度。选择审计显示受控复杂度，最大验证遗憾为4.30%。部署时保留展开时间0.003秒至0.242秒，Beaver Bayou实测对比给出估计2.75×10^4倍水平归一化加速比。

Conclusion: 当前原生细胞增量是强大的求解器条件预测支架，添加的响应场、神经或空间复杂度应仅在经验验证合理时才予保留。

Abstract: This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.

</details>


### [215] [Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06393)
*Jef Jonkers,Glenn Van Wallendael,Luc Duchateau,Sofie Van Hoecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对右删失生存数据的通分评分规则框架：先将预测分布通过删失机制映射为观测数据分布，再应用标准评分规则，从而得到删失版CRPS、Brier分数等。该框架在条件独立删失下是恰当的，并导出了删失回归（censored engression）这一多变量生存建模的学习目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 右删失数据下事件时间仅部分观测，标准通分评分规则无法直接应用，需要构建适用于删失场景的评分规则框架。

Method: 将预测分布经删失机制映射为观测数据分布，再对观测数据分布应用标准评分规则；对于固定删失时间得到局部化分数，对于随机/部分观测删失时间得到边缘化分数。

Result: 该框架恢复并统一了右删失似然和IPCW准则，给出了CRPS、pinball损失、Brier分数和能量分数的右删失版本；在条件独立删失下边缘化分数是恰当的，在可识别区域上严格恰当；实验表明其正确排序了预言机预测，而依赖预测的加权分数会出现排序反转。

Conclusion: 所提删失评分框架在理论上保证恰当性，实践中避免了加权评分的排序反转问题，并自然推广到多变量生存建模的删失回归方法。

Abstract: Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.

</details>


### [216] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出曲率分层评估框架（CurvBench），揭示关系学习中模型性能对数据内在几何结构的依赖性，证明传统聚合排行榜存在系统性偏差，并基于14个数据集、18个模型的实验发现模型排名在曲率域内高度稳定但跨域显著变化，最终提出几何感知评估协议以取代标准聚合基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关系学习评估依赖跨异构数据集的平均性能排行榜，隐含地假设均匀的底层结构。这种假设引入系统性偏差：掩盖几何相关的性能差异，并可能导致关于模型泛化的误导性结论。本文旨在识别数据内在几何作为模型效能的关键潜在因素，并解决聚合指标掩盖关键性能权衡的问题。

Method: 1. 识别内在几何（曲率）作为影响模型性能的潜在因素；2. 提出曲率分层评估框架，将数据集划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三个区间；3. 在14个数据集上评估18个代表性模型（包括GCNs、GFMs和表格学习方法）；4. 分析模型排名在不同曲率区间内的稳定性及跨区间的变化；5. 提出几何感知评估协议。

Result: 1. 模型排名在各自曲率区间内高度稳定，但在不同区间之间显著变化，表明性能本质上依赖于几何而非通用可迁移；2. 识别出GFMs在某些曲率区间中相比几何对齐的GNNs收益递减；3. 证实传统聚合指标掩盖了关键的几何相关性能权衡。

Conclusion: 传统聚合排行榜存在系统性偏差，需采用曲率分层的几何感知评估协议以获得更可靠且可解释的模型比较。研究为未来关系学习方法提供了可复现的曲率分层数据集拆分和评估工具。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>


### [217] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，结合梯度预条件和激活预条件，专门解决深度学习中测试时反馈（TTF）问题——即训练/验证损失与下游指标（如任务成功率、生成质量）之间的失配，特别是在长序列任务中。实验表明DoPr能提升下游性能，但验证损失改善并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多深度学习应用（如自回归语言建模、流生成模型、机器人策略学习）使用单步预测损失训练，但部署时沿自身预测滚动执行，导致训练与测试间的分布偏移（即TTF现象），且随任务长度加剧。现有方法多从数据、架构、目标函数入手，本文提出从优化角度缓解误差累积。

Method: 提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，将Adam/Muon的梯度级预条件与KFAC等激活级预条件相结合，形成联合预条件优化方法，专门针对TTF场景设计。

Result: 在多种TTF设定下，DoPr作为即插即用方法提升了下游模型性能。有趣的是，这些测试时性能增益并不总是伴随验证损失的改善，暗示了现有评估方式的局限性。

Conclusion: 从优化角度（而非数据、架构或目标函数）缓解TTF误差累积是可行的新方向。DoPr的有效性及验证损失与下游指标的不一致性，提出了关于如何正确评估单步监督训练模型的新问题。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [218] [Causal Atlases from Entropic Inference: Bayesian Networks beyond Optimal DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06440)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵推断的方法生成因果图谱集，揭示优化得到的DAG可能包含与数据不一致的因果伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯网络构建依赖优化方法，但底层数据可能允许多条因果链，优化方法不适合学习存在内在变异性的因果关系。

Method: 在2节点和20节点线性结构方程模型的模拟噪声数据上，采样最大熵图集成，量化因果关系的结构模糊性。

Result: 优化得到的DAG可能包含等价精确拓扑中不一致的因果伪影；熵基方法能生成与数据一致的多个因果图谱。

Conclusion: 熵基推断能够构建更忠实于数据变异性的因果表示，避免优化方法引入的伪影。

Abstract: Data-driven causal relationship identification is pertinent to advancing understanding of complex systems both within and beyond science. Bayesian networks offer a probabilistic method for modelling generic causal relationships via directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). However, typical techniques for constructing Bayesian networks rely on optimization, which can be ill-suited for learning causal relationships because the underlying data may admit multiple chains of causation. More data-faithful representations of causal relationships would provide frameworks for constructing multiple causal maps that are consistent with the variability that is inherent in underlying data. Here, we show that entropy-based inference generates atlases of plausible causal relationships that are consistent with underlying data. On simulated noisy data of 2- and 20-node linear structural equation models, we sample a maximum-entropy ensemble of graphs that allow us to quantify the inherent structural ambiguity in underlying causal relationships. Our method shows that "optimized" DAGs can contain causal artifacts are not consistent across equivalently accurate topologies.

</details>


### [219] [In-Context Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458)
*Alexander Möllers,Marvin Sextro,Julius Hense,Gabriel Dernbach,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习器，通过在合成数据上预训练，能够从少量标注包中解决新的MIL任务，无需梯度更新即可单次前向传播完成分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL算法在低标注场景下表现不佳：灵活模型易过拟合，刚性模型无法适应任务。

Method: 使用Perceiver架构构建上下文学习器，在合成数据上进行预训练；设计了多种面向包结构数据的合成数据生成器，并采用混合训练策略。

Result: 在12个MIL基准测试中，混合预训练模型平均表现最佳，超越了需要任务特定训练的有监督基线。

Conclusion: 合成数据混合预训练能赋予模型互补的归纳偏置，使其在少量标注包下高效泛化到新任务。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.

</details>


### [220] [Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06459)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AI-RAN/O-RAN架构中多个AI驱动控制功能相互干扰的问题，提出了一种将噪声连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指标的方法，用于检测控制参数与网络性能指标之间的真实依赖关系。通过合成闭环流量生成器评估机器学习恢复流水线，实验表明该方法在信号足够强时能可靠恢复潜在依赖结构，阈值校准是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络（AI-RAN/O-RAN）中多个AI驱动控制功能同时优化不同网络目标时，它们之间的相互干扰难以从原始网络数据中检测。现有方法缺乏一个可靠、可解释的依赖结构来捕获控制参数与网络性能结果之间的主动影响关系。

Method: 将连续遥测数据转换为二值事件指标的问题建模为显著性检测问题；引入合成闭环流量生成器，其中植入了已知的潜在依赖关系作为基准真值；设计并评估基于机器学习的依赖恢复流水线。

Result: 当信号与背景变化的分离度足够高时，所提出的流水线能够可靠地从含噪连续轨迹中恢复潜在依赖结构；阈值校准被识别为控制事件检测质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该方法为自适应AI-RAN控制系统实现可解释的依赖学习奠定了基础步骤，为后续管理多AI控制功能间的交互提供了关键支撑。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [221] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种预条件（PC）层，通过多项式预条件子对权重矩阵进行参数化，确保LLM训练过程中权重条件数的稳定，训练后可合并回原始架构，不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练中权重矩阵的条件数不稳定会导致优化困难，现有方法缺乏对权重谱的显式控制手段。

Method: 通过低阶多项式预条件子重塑权重矩阵的奇异值谱，将PC层作为权重参数化模块嵌入训练过程。

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练中，PC层在AdamW和Muon两种优化器下均优于标准Transformer；理论证明在深度线性网络中，均匀约束每层奇异值可保证梯度下降几何收敛到全局最小值。

Conclusion: PC层能有效稳定权重条件数、提升训练效果，且无推理开销，具有理论和实践双重优势。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [222] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度线性网络中多路径的竞争机制，发现大步长离散梯度下降（GD）在稳定性边缘的振荡会打破梯度流（GF）预测的"胜者通吃"专业化现象，转而促使信号在多个路径间重新均衡分布，且这种再平衡效应随深度和路径数量增加而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于梯度流（GF）的分析预测多路径深度线性网络会呈现"胜者通吃"的专业化模式，但实际训练中观察到的现象与这一预测不符，作者旨在揭示离散梯度下降（GD）与梯度流之间的关键差异，特别是大步长对路径竞争的影响。

Method: 通过理论证明单路径解是尖锐极小值，而多路径分布信号能降低尖锐度（锐度因子随路径数和深度增加而减小）；分析早期训练（深度驱动的对称性破缺）与后期稳定性边缘（Edge of Stability）振荡的动力学过程。

Result: 早期训练复制了GF预测的深度驱动对称性破缺，但随后稳定性边缘的振荡覆盖了这一趋势，驱动网络进入再平衡阶段，信号在各路径间重新分布。

Conclusion: 深度塑造了路径竞争的方式，大步长GD倾向于产生共享表示而非持续的单一路径主导，这解释了实际训练中观察到的多路径协作现象。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [223] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO等强化学习方法在推理语言模型中存在的延迟奖励和高方差问题，本文提出RREDCoT方法，利用模型自身进行最优奖励重分配，无需额外生成，实现对思维链轨迹中重要片段的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
